[?2004l[?2004h> import openpyxl,re
[?2004l[?2004h> wb=openpyxl.load_workbook('/workspace/input.xlsx')
[?2004l[?2004h> for ws in wb.worksheets:
[?2004l[?2004h>     byrow={}
[?2004l[?2004h>     for row in ws.iter_rows():
[?2004l[?2004h>         cells=[]
[?2004l[?2004h>         for c in row:
[?2004l[?2004h>             if isinstance(c.value,str) and c.value.startswith('='):
[?2004l[?2004h>                 cells.append((c.coordinate,c.value))
[?2004l[?2004h>         if cells: byrow[c.row]=cells
[?2004l[?2004h>     inhom=[]
[?2004l[?2004h>     for r,cells in sorted(byrow.items()):
[?2004l[?2004h>         patts={}
[?2004l[?2004h>         for co,v in cells:
[?2004l[?2004h>             p=re.sub(r'[A-Z]{1,3}\d+','@',v)
[?2004l[?2004h>             patts.setdefault(p,[]).append((co,v))
[?2004l[?2004h>         if len(patts)>1:
[?2004l[?2004h>             inhom.append((r,len(cells),patts))
[?2004l[?2004h>     if inhom:
[?2004l[?2004h>         print('===',ws.title, 'inhom rows', len(inhom))
[?2004l[?2004h>         for r,n,patts in inhom:
[?2004l[?2004h>             print('R',r,'n',n)
[?2004l[?2004h>             for p,lst in list(patts.items())[:6]:
[?2004l[?2004h>                 print(' ',lst[0][0],lst[0][1])
[?2004l[?2004h>                 if len(lst)>1:
[?2004l[?2004h>                     print('  last',lst[-1][0],lst[-1][1])
[?2004l[?2004h> PY

[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Construction','S33'): '=R35',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Construction','X34'): '=X27',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Construction','Q39'): "=(('Input Assumptions'!$D$35+'Input Assumptions'!$D$36)*Q33*TBA!P14)",
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Construction','AN39'): "=('Input Assumptions'!$D$35+'Input Assumptions'!$D$36)*AN33*TBA!AM14",
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Construction','AC43'): '=AC16-AC28<0.01',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('P&L','G11'): '=-Operation!F21',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('P&L','I11'): '=-Operation!H21',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('P&L','AH13'): '=SUM(AH9:AH12)',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('P&L','W15'): '=Amortization!V13',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('P&L','AJ15'): '=Amortization!AI13',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('P&L','AO15'): '=Amortization!AN13',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('P&L','M21'): '=SUM(M17:M20)',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('CFS','X12'): "='P&L'!X23",
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('CFS','AP12'): "='P&L'!AP23",
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('CFS','AJ12'): "='P&L'!AJ23",
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('CFS','Y14'): '=SUM(Y8:Y13)',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('CFS','P19'): '=P14+P16+P17',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('CFS','S19'): '=S14+S16+S17',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Ratios','U8'): '=-Construction!T12',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Ratios','AD10'): '=-Construction!AC14',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Ratios','AE11'): '=-Construction!AD15',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Ratios','AO24'): '=-Construction!AN26',
[?2004l[?2004h>  ('Ratios','H27'): '=SUM(H24:H26)',
[?2004l[?2004h> }
[?2004l[?2004h> for (sheet, coord), formula in fixes.items():
[?2004l[?2004h>     ws = wb[sheet]
[?2004l[?2004h>     old = ws[coord].value
[?2004l[?2004h>     ws[coord] = formula
[?2004l[?2004h>     print(sheet, coord, repr(old), '->', repr(formula))
